Calcutta, the 27th November 1954

RESERVE BANK OF INDIA 

NOTIFICATION 

Bombay, the 17th November 1954 
No. 19— Shri P. K. Banerjee is posted as Assistant 
Accountant, Reserve Bank ol India, Inspection Depar 1001 
Central Office, Bombay with effect from 28th October 19o4. 

2 Sbri K L. Anand, Assistant Accountant, Reserve Bank 
«f India, Department of Banking Development, Bombay 
proceeded on leave with effect from 28th Octobei 1954. 

3 Shri P. S. Mokashi, Deputy Chief Officer, Reserve 
Bank of India, Department of Banking Operations, Bombay 
is transferred in the same capacity to the Reserve Bank of 
India, Department of Banking Operations, Calcutta with 
effect’ from 1st November 1954. 

4 Shri R P Iyer is appointed to officiate as Banking 
Officer Reserve Bank of India, Department of Banking 
Operations, Trivandrum with effect from 1st November 
1954 vice Shri S. Philipose proceeded on leave. 

5 On return from leave Shri A, Bhattacharyya is repost- 
ed as officiating Banking Officer, Reserve Bank of India, 
Department of Banking Operations, Madras with effect 
from 1st November 1954 vice Shri P. Rajanarayanan 
reverted as Superintendent. 

6. Shri S. N. Sen, officiating Assistant Currency Officer, 
Reserve Bank of India, Issue Department, Calcutta revert- 
ed as Superintendent with effect from the close of business 
on 1st November 1954. 

7 On return from leave Shri K. M. De is reposted as 
Accountant, Reserve Bank of India, Delhi with effect from 
the close of business on 2nd November 1954 vice Shri G. S. 
TCbbli reverted as Superintendent. 


8. On return from leave Shri U. M. Banerjee is reposted 
as Banking Officer, Reserve Bank of India, Department of 
Banking Operations, Bombay with effect from 4th Novem- 
ber 1954. 


9. Shri O. P. Berry is appointed to officiate as Banking 
Officer, Reserve Bank of India, Department of Banking 
Operations, Trivandrum with effect from 8th November 
1954 vice Shri K. Vasudeva Rao proceeded on leave. 

10. Shri V, G. Wagle is appointed to officiate as Deputy 
Chief Accountant, Reserve Bank of India, Central. Office, 
Bombay with effect from the close of business on 8th Nov- 
ember 1954 vice Shri K. N, Mehta proceeded on leave. 

11. Mr. G. E. Watson, officiating Accountant, Reserve 
Bank of India, London proceeded on leave with effect from 
9th November 1954. 


12. Shri M. Sadanandan is appointed to officiate as Bank- 
ing Officer, Reserve Bank of India, Department of Banking 
Operations, Trivandrum with effect from 10th November 
1954 vice Shri K. Raghavan proceeded on leave. 

13, On return from leave Shri K, Venkatarama Ayyar is 
posted as Banking Officer, Reserve Bank of India, Depart- 
ment of Banking Operations, Madras with effect from 11th 
November 1954. 


14. On return from leave Shri P. S. Sitaram is posted as 

Assistant Chief Officer, Reserve Bank of India, Department 
of Banking Operations, Delhi with effect from the close of 
business on 11th November 1954 vice Shri B. N. Malhotra 
proceeded on leave. 


RAM NATH 
Deputy Governor
